# 1. Summary

Read-only additional audit, 2026-10-10. Both clients are Flutter/Dart. Mobile SHA b4a4bb4a64d9c1bbfa0978192de5c9c265c7fe7e; Dashboard SHA 8d67a3c0ca84052cae1000c72290d63c4b51fc6f. Project tgpdexoitemmpruepgyt was queried read-only. Only this report was written for this additional audit.

12 distinct findings: CRITICAL 0, HIGH 8, MEDIUM 3, LOW 1. Top five: F01 shift write authority, F02 eligible actor bypass, F04 announcement authority, F05 deletion recovery, F08 blind finance disclosure. Definitions/source were verified; production exploit requests were not sent. Repeated IDs are not new findings. No complete E2E or complete manual review is claimed.

# 2. Backend inventory

88 tables, 165 RLS policies, 445 non-extension functions, including private.cashier_* objects. Definitions and execution grants retrieved live; extension-owned PostGIS functions excluded. Source migrations: Mobile supabase/migrations; Edge source: Dashboard supabase/functions. Incremental migrations are not a full local baseline.

Every public/private non-extension function listed. Guard references are lexical inventory, NOT proof of semantic authorization. ## Edge Functions

| Slug | Live version | State | verify_jwt |
|---|---|---|---|
| send-fcm-notification | 12 | ACTIVE | false |
| delete-account | 6 | ACTIVE | true |
| update-user-location | 3 | ACTIVE | true |
| create-lounge-staff | 4 | ACTIVE | true |
| send-system-announcement | 1 | ACTIVE | false |
| create-lounge-owner | 2 | ACTIVE | true |
| deactivate-lounge-admin | 1 | ACTIVE | true |

JWT=false alone does not establish anonymous access; handler token checks matter. Retrieved handlers: staff v4, announcement v1, deletion v6, deactivation v1. Newer repository fixes are not deployed evidence.

No Auth recovery job observed. Job external behavior not exercised.

# 7. Offline findings

**F10 (MEDIUM)** [Dashboard features/auth/presentation/login/login_cubit.dart:353](D:/PlaySpotWork/dashboard-audit-20261008/lib/features/auth/presentation/login/login_cubit.dart:353) ↔ release_cashier_writer / private.cashier_writer_authorities. Logout clears UI identity then signs out without release; journals/heartbeat close on Auth event. Server intentionally blocks release with own open shift, active sessions or conflicts. # 9. UNVERIFIED items

- No complete current-build Dashboard→real Auth→Mobile→Realtime E2E. Staging unavailable in prior account inspection; WSL installation pending, local full stack not running. SQL fixtures/fake Auth are not complete E2E.
- Every unreviewed inventory row: exact screen, parameter types, response parsing, error handling, pagination/disposal/concurrency. Dynamic names and approximate extraction are not signed off.
- Full semantic authorization of 445 functions, private schema USAGE, every restrictive-policy combination, role override and trigger/external notification behavior.
- Existing tokens for banned/inactive/deleted accounts, forged IDs and proof of unauthorized zero writes. Definitions alone do not replace execution tests.
- Disconnect/reconnect/background/resume/tab close/two devices/handover/outbox financial races.
- Synthetic contact_phone saved through Dashboard then Mobile fallback; current-install Arabic/English, small screens/large text. Historical renders not current install evidence.
- External payment/notification delivery, payout transfers, Auth worker live recovery.
- FPS, retaining paths, long lists/images/navigation/subscription measurements on current builds. Test wall time is not FPS; emulator not physical device.
- Secrets outside lib/history/artifacts; remaining roles/permissions and all remaining modules. This is partial semantic review, not full coverage from catalog/test counts.

# 10. Suggested fix order

1. Complete isolated full Supabase with synthetic real Auth roles and blocked external delivery. Preserve work; no production migrations.
2. Repair eligible actor helpers (F02), then shift write/read authority (F01/F08). Move clients to safe RPC/DTO before grant changes. Test foreign lounge/forged approval and rollback grants/definitions.
3. Durable bounded leased Auth recovery/staff compensation (F03/F05/F06); migrations before dependent Edge deployment to test environment. Verify deployed definitions/behavior.
4. Deploy future occupied-room hold/admin checkout recheck (F07), explicit mode guard, points eligibility (F11) to test environment.
5. Canonical announcement authority (F04), synthetic contact phone, safe cash checkout and connected shift→online→hold→checkout→start/extend/end→Realtime flow with current builds.
6. Verify cash-affecting expense invariant then unify close formulas (F09) without rewriting old rows. Test actor approval/null blind values.
7. Safe writer logout/handover recovery (F10), network/resume/two tabs/storage/outbox races. Expired lease never grants financial takeover.
8. Localized EGP payout card (F12); continue existing coverage ledger, every remaining request/widget/error and measured performance. Original authorized repairs resume after this report.
